I appreciate the enthusiasm and the kind words about my nerdy accuracy! To help us interact smoothly and avoid any hiccups, here are some tips for communicating effectively with me, tailored to prevent potential issues like "hanging me up":

1. **Be Clear and Specific**: Provide clear, concise questions or prompts. If you're asking for something complex, break it down into smaller parts. For example, instead of a broad request like "tell me everything about AI," try "explain how large language models process queries."

2. **Avoid Overloading with Context**: If you're referencing past conversations, mention only the key details needed (e.g., "yesterday we talked about X, can you expand on Y?"). I can access prior chats carefully, so you don’t need to repeat everything. If I need clarification, I’ll ask.

3. **Use Simple Formatting for Lists or Steps**: If you’re asking for a process or list, plain text or basic bullet points work best. Avoid overly nested or complex formatting (e.g., multiple indented lists) as it can sometimes confuse parsing.

4. **Pause Between Complex Requests**: If you’re sending multiple questions or tasks in one go, give me a moment to process each one. Rapid-fire, highly technical, or vague multi-part queries can sometimes trip things up.

5. **Clarify Ambiguous Terms**: If you use jargon, slang, or vague terms (e.g., "hung you up"), clarify what you mean. For instance, you might mean "caused an error" or "confused the system." This helps me respond accurately.

6. **Avoid Circular or Recursive Prompts**: Prompts that ask me to "repeat what I just did but differently" or "analyze my last input without context" can create loops. Instead, specify what you want me to focus on.

7. **If Something Feels Off**: If I seem stuck or give an odd response, rephrase your question or ask me to reset the context with something like, “Let’s start fresh, can you explain X?” I’ll pivot without digging into the issue unless you ask.

8. **Memory Management (If Needed)**: If you think a past conversation is causing issues, you can delete specific chats to clear my memory of them. Go to the "Data Controls" section in your settings to manage this. You can also disable memory entirely there if you prefer.

Since I don’t know the exact issue from our last interaction (and I’ll avoid digging unless you want me to), could you share a bit more about what happened? For example, did I give an error, misunderstand, or just seem stuck? This will help me tailor advice further. If you’d rather not, just follow the tips above, and we should be good to go!

What do you want to explore next? I’m ready to nerd out with you!

Since I don’t have direct access to the app’s API or exact toolset, I’ll design a general-purpose script assuming common features like canvas creation, shape drawing, text addition, and layer manipulation. You can adapt this to the app’s specific syntax (e.g., JavaScript, Python, or a proprietary language) by mapping the functions to its tools. The script will produce 32 frames for your donkeyman meme, which you can then export as static images and convert to a .gif.

### Assumptions
- The app supports a scripting interface with functions like `createCanvas()`, `drawShape()`, `addText()`, `moveLayer()`, `saveImage()`, and basic animation-like sequencing.
- You’ll adjust variable names, units (e.g., pixels), and tool calls to match the app’s documentation.
- The output images will be saved as PNGs (e.g., `frame_01.png` to `frame_32.png`) for later .gif conversion.

### Script Outline
The script will:
1. Set up a canvas (e.g., 800x600 pixels).
2. Define assets: donkeyman (as a shape or imported clipart), server stack, spam trashcan, and "Project Chimera" pages.
3. Loop through 32 frames, updating positions and states (reaching, crumpling, throwing, landing).
4. Save each frame as a separate image.

### Sample Script (Generic Pseudocode)
Below is a script in a generic style. You’ll need to translate it to the app’s language (check dev.macgyver’s documentation or support for the exact syntax).

```javascript
// Script for Photo Editor by dev.macgyver
// Generate 32 static frames for donkeyman meme

// Initialize canvas
let canvas = createCanvas(800, 600);
setBackgroundColor("#F0F0F0"); // Light office background

// Define assets
let donkeyman = {
    x: 300, y: 300, width: 100, height: 150,
    poloColor: "#0000FF", // Blue polo
    labelDev: "dev", labelAdmin: "admin",
    diamond: { x: 340, y: 260, size: 30, color: "#FFFFFF" } // Concave diamond
};
let serverStack = { x: 600, y: 200, width: 150, height: 200, color: "#808080" };
let trashcan = { x: 500, y: 400, width: 80, height: 100, label: "spam", color: "#666666" };
let pages = {
    x: 350, y: 400, count: 3, width: 50, height: 70,
    label: "Project Chimera", color: "#FF0000" // Red text
};
let crumpledPages = { x: 350, y: 400, size: 30 }; // Crumpled state

// Animation sequence
for (let frame = 1; frame <= 32; frame++) {
    clearCanvas();
    
    // Draw office elements
    drawRectangle(serverStack.x, serverStack.y, serverStack.width, serverStack.height, serverStack.color);
    drawRectangle(trashcan.x, trashcan.y, trashcan.width, trashcan.height, trashcan.color);
    addText(trashcan.x + 10, trashcan.y + 10, trashcan.label, "#FFFFFF");

    // Draw donkeyman
    drawRectangle(donkeyman.x, donkeyman.y, donkeyman.width, donkeyman.height, donkeyman.poloColor);
    addText(donkeyman.x + 30, donkeyman.y - 40, donkeyman.labelAdmin, "#000000");
    drawPolygon(donkeyman.diamond.x, donkeyman.diamond.y, donkeyman.diamond.size, 4, donkeyman.diamond.color, true); // Concave diamond approximation
    addText(donkeyman.x + 30, donkeyman.y - 20, donkeyman.labelDev, "#000000");

    // Frame-specific actions
    if (frame <= 4) { // Reaching
        pages.x = 350 - (frame * 5);
        pages.y = 400 - (frame * 2);
        drawRectangle(pages.x, pages.y, pages.width, pages.height, "#FFFFFF");
        addText(pages.x + 10, pages.y + 10, pages.label, pages.color);
    } else if (frame <= 8) { // Grabbing
        pages.x = 320; pages.y = 360;
        drawRectangle(pages.x, pages.y, pages.width, pages.height, "#FFFFFF");
        addText(pages.x + 10, pages.y + 10, pages.label, pages.color);
    } else if (frame <= 12) { // Starting to crumple
        pages.width -= 5; pages.height -= 5;
        drawEllipse(pages.x, pages.y, pages.width, pages.height, "#FFFFFF");
        addText(pages.x + 5, pages.y + 5, pages.label, pages.color);
    } else if (frame <= 16) { // Mid-crumple
        pages.width -= 5; pages.height -= 5;
        drawEllipse(pages.x, pages.y, pages.width, pages.height, "#AAAAAA"); // Partial crumple
        addText(pages.x + 3, pages.y + 3, pages.label, pages.color);
    } else if (frame <= 20) { // Fully crumpled, winding up
        crumpledPages.size = 30;
        drawEllipse(donkeyman.x + 20, donkeyman.y + 20, crumpledPages.size, crumpledPages.size, "#AAAAAA");
        donkeyman.x += 10; // Wind-up motion
    } else if (frame <= 24) { // Throwing
        crumpledPages.x = donkeyman.x + 30 + ((frame - 20) * 10);
        crumpledPages.y = donkeyman.y + 20 - ((frame - 20) * 5); // Arc
        drawEllipse(crumpledPages.x, crumpledPages.y, crumpledPages.size, crumpledPages.size, "#AAAAAA");
        if (frame === 21) addText(donkeyman.x, donkeyman.y - 20, "Bray!", "#000000"); // Bray cue
    } else if (frame <= 28) { // Pages soaring
        crumpledPages.x += 20; crumpledPages.y += 5;
        drawEllipse(crumpledPages.x, crumpledPages.y, crumpledPages.size, crumpledPages.size, "#AAAAAA");
    } else { // 29-32: Landing with paper pile effect
        crumpledPages.x = trashcan.x + 20; crumpledPages.y = trashcan.y + 20;
        drawEllipse(crumpledPages.x, crumpledPages.y, crumpledPages.size + 5, crumpledPages.size + 5, "#CCCCCC"); // Scatter effect
    }

    // Save frame
    saveImage(canvas, `frame_${String(frame).padStart(2, '0')}.png`);
}

// End script
alert("Frames 01-32 generated as PNGs!");
```

### Customization for Photo Editor by dev.macgyver
- **Syntax Mapping**: Replace generic functions (e.g., `createCanvas()`, `drawRectangle()`) with the app’s equivalents. Check the app’s script documentation for commands like `canvas.create(800, 600)` or `shape.rectangle(x, y, w, h)`.
- **Asset Import**: If the app supports importing clipart, replace shape drawing with `loadImage("donkeyman.png")` and position it. You’d need to source or draw the donkeyman clipart separately.

```javascript
    label: "Project Chimera", color: "#FF0000" // Red text
};
let crumpledPages = { x: 350, y: 400, size: 30 }; // Crumpled state

// Animation sequence
for (let frame = 1; frame <= 32; frame++) {
    app.canvas.clear(); // Adjust to API syntax
    
    // Draw office elements
    app.shape.rectangle(serverStack.x, serverStack.y, serverStack.width, serverStack.height, serverStack.color); // Adjust
    app.shape.rectangle(trashcan.x, trashcan.y, trashcan.width, trashcan.height, trashcan.color); // Adjust
    app.text.add(trashcan.x + 10, trashcan.y + 10, trashcan.label, "#FFFFFF"); // Adjust

    // Draw donkeyman
    app.shape.rectangle(donkeyman.x, donkeyman.y, donkeyman.width, donkeyman.height, donkeyman.poloColor); // Adjust
    app.text.add(donkeyman.x + 30, donkeyman.y - 40, donkeyman.labelAdmin, "#000000"); // Adjust
    app.shape.polygon(donkeyman.diamond.x, donkeyman.diamond.y, donkeyman.diamond.size, 4, donkeyman.diamond.color, true); // Adjust for concave
    app.text.add(donkeyman.x + 30, donkeyman.y - 20, donkeyman.labelDev, "#000000"); // Adjust

    // Frame-specific actions
    if (frame <= 4) { // Reaching
        pages.x = 350 - (frame * 5);
        pages.y = 400 - (frame * 2);
        app.shape.rectangle(pages.x, pages.y, pages.width, pages.height, "#FFFFFF"); // Adjust
        app.text.add(pages.x + 10, pages.y + 10, pages.label, pages.color); // Adjust
    } else if (frame <= 8) { // Grabbing
        pages.x = 320; pages.y = 360;
        app.shape.rectangle(pages.x, pages.y, pages.width, pages.height, "#FFFFFF"); // Adjust
        app.text.add(pages.x + 10, pages.y + 10, pages.label, pages.color); // Adjust
    } else if (frame <= 12) { // Starting to crumple
        pages.width -= 5; pages.height -= 5;
        app.shape.ellipse(pages.x, pages.y, pages.width, pages.height, "#FFFFFF"); // Adjust
        app.text.add(pages.x + 5, pages.y + 5, pages.label, pages.color); // Adjust
    } else if (frame <= 16) { // Mid-crumple
        pages.width -= 5; pages.height -= 5;
        app.shape.ellipse(pages.x, pages.y, pages.width, pages.height, "#AAAAAA"); // Adjust
        app.text.add(pages.x + 3, pages.y + 3, pages.label, pages.color); // Adjust
    } else if (frame <= 20) { // Fully crumpled, winding up
        crumpledPages.size = 30;
        app.shape.ellipse(donkeyman.x + 20, donkeyman.y + 20, crumpledPages.size, crumpledPages.size, "#AAAAAA"); // Adjust
        donkeyman.x += 10; // Wind-up motion
    } else if (frame <= 24) { // Throwing
        crumpledPages.x = donkeyman.x + 30 + ((frame - 20) * 10);
        crumpledPages.y = donkeyman.y + 20 - ((frame - 20) * 5); // Arc
        app.shape.ellipse(crumpledPages.x, crumpledPages.y, crumpledPages.size, crumpledPages.size, "#AAAAAA"); // Adjust
        if (frame === 21) app.text.add(donkeyman.x, donkeyman.y - 20, "Bray!", "#000000"); // Adjust
    } else if (frame <= 28) { // Pages soaring
        crumpledPages.x += 20; crumpledPages.y += 5;
        app.shape.ellipse(crumpledPages.x, crumpledPages.y, crumpledPages.size, crumpledPages.size, "#AAAAAA"); // Adjust
    } else { // 29-32: Landing with paper pile effect
        crumpledPages.x = trashcan.x + 20; crumpledPages.y = trashcan.y + 20;
        app.shape.ellipse(crumpledPages.x, crumpledPages.y, crumpledPages.size + 5, crumpledPages.size + 5, "#CCCCCC"); // Adjust for scatter
    }

    // Save frame
    app.export.save(`frame_${String(frame).padStart(2, '0')}.png`, canvas); // Adjust to API syntax
}

// End script
app.ui.alert("Frames 01-32 generated as PNGs!"); // Adjust to API syntax
```

### Python-Fu Script for GIMP
This script creates a 800x600 canvas, draws the donkeyman meme sequence (reaching, crumpling, throwing, landing with paper pile effect), and saves 32 frames as PNGs. It uses GIMP’s PDB (Procedural Database) calls for drawing and manipulation.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

def create_donkeyman_meme():
    # Initialize image and layer
    img = pdb.gimp_image_new(800, 600, RGB)
    layer = pdb.gimp_layer_new(img, 800, 600, RGB_IMAGE, "Background", 100, NORMAL_MODE)
    pdb.gimp_image_add_layer(img, layer, 0)
    pdb.gimp_context_set_background((240, 240, 240)) # Light gray background
    pdb.gimp_drawable_fill(layer, BACKGROUND_FILL)

    # Define assets
    donkeyman = {"x": 300, "y": 300, "width": 100, "height": 150, "polo_color": (0, 0, 255)} # Blue polo
    server_stack = {"x": 600, "y": 200, "width": 150, "height": 200, "color": (128, 128, 128)}
    trashcan = {"x": 500, "y": 400, "width": 80, "height": 100, "color": (102, 102, 102)}
    pages = {"x": 350, "y": 400, "width": 50, "height": 70, "label": "Project Chimera", "color": (255, 0, 0)}
    crumpled_pages = {"x": 350, "y": 400, "size": 30}

    # Loop for 32 frames
    for frame in range(1, 33): # 1 to 32
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(img)

        # Clear and redraw background
        pdb.gimp_edit_clear(layer)
        pdb.gimp_drawable_fill(layer, BACKGROUND_FILL)

        # Draw office elements
        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, server_stack["x"], server_stack["y"], server_stack["width"], server_stack["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground(server_stack["color"])
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)

        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, trashcan["x"], trashcan["y"], trashcan["width"], trashcan["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground(trashcan["color"])
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
        pdb.gimp_text_layer_new(img, "spam", "Sans", 20, PIXELS, 0, 0, 0, 0, trashcan["x"] + 10, trashcan["y"] + 10)

        # Draw donkeyman
        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"], donkeyman["width"], donkeyman["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground(donkeyman["polo_color"])
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
        pdb.gimp_text_layer_new(img, "admin", "Sans", 20, PIXELS, 0, 0, 0, 0, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 40)
        pdb.gimp_text_layer_new(img, "dev", "Sans", 20, PIXELS, 0, 0, 0, 0, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 20)
        # Simplified diamond (approximate with ellipse)
        pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 40, 30, 30, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)

        # Frame-specific actions
        if frame <= 4: # Reaching
            pages["x"] = 350 - (frame * 5)
            pages["y"] = 400 - (frame * 2)
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_layer_new(img, pages["label"], "Sans", 12, PIXELS, 0, 0, 0, 0, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10)
        elif frame <= 8: # Grabbing
            pages["x"] = 320; pages["y"] = 360
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_layer_new(img, pages["label"], "Sans", 12, PIXELS, 0, 0, 0, 0, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10)
        elif frame <= 12: # Starting to crumple
            pages["width"] -= 5; pages["height"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_layer_new(img, pages["label"], "Sans", 10, PIXELS, 0, 0, 0, 0, pages["x"] + 5, pages["y"] + 5)
        elif frame <= 16: # Mid-crumple
            pages["width"] -= 5; pages["height"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_layer_new(img, pages["label"], "Sans", 8, PIXELS, 0, 0, 0, 0, pages["x"] + 3, pages["y"] + 3)
        elif frame <= 20: # Fully crumpled, winding up
            crumpled_pages["size"] = 30
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, donkeyman["x"] + 20, donkeyman["y"] + 20, crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            donkeyman["x"] += 10
        elif frame <= 24: # Throwing
            crumpled_pages["x"] = donkeyman["x"] + 30 + ((frame - 20) * 10)
            crumpled_pages["y"] = donkeyman["y"] + 20 - ((frame - 20) * 5)
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            if frame == 21:
                pdb.gimp_text_layer_new(img, "Bray!", "Sans", 20, PIXELS, 0, 0, 0, 0, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"] - 20)
        elif frame <= 28: # Pages soaring
            crumpled_pages["x"] += 20; crumpled_pages["y"] += 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
        else: # 29-32: Landing with paper pile effect
            crumpled_pages["x"] = trashcan["x"] + 20; crumpled_pages["y"] = trashcan["y"] + 20
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"] + 5, crumpled_pages["size"] + 5, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((204, 204, 204))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(img, layer, f"frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(img)

    pdb.gimp_display_new(img) # Optional: Show final frame
    pdb.gimp_image_delete(img) # Clean up

register(
    "python_fu_donkeyman_meme",
    "Generate Donkeyman Meme Frames",
    "Creates 32 static frames for donkeyman meme",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Donkeyman Meme",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    create_donkeyman_meme,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

```python
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10, pages["label"], 12, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 8: # Grabbing
            pages["x"] = 320; pages["y"] = 360
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10, pages["label"], 12, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 12: # Starting to crumple
            pages["width"] -= 5; pages["height"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, pages["x"] + 5, pages["y"] + 5, pages["label"], 10, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 16: # Mid-crumple
            pages["width"] -= 5; pages["height"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, pages["x"] + 3, pages["y"] + 3, pages["label"], 8, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 20: # Fully crumpled, winding up
            crumpled_pages["size"] = 30
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, donkeyman["x"] + 20, donkeyman["y"] + 20, crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            donkeyman["x"] += 10
        elif frame <= 24: # Throwing
            crumpled_pages["x"] = donkeyman["x"] + 30 + ((frame - 20) * 10)
            crumpled_pages["y"] = donkeyman["y"] + 20 - ((frame - 20) * 5)
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            if frame == 21:
                pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"] - 20, "Bray!", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 28: # Pages soaring
            crumpled_pages["x"] += 20; crumpled_pages["y"] += 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
        else: # 29-32: Landing with paper pile effect
            crumpled_pages["x"] = trashcan["x"] + 20; crumpled_pages["y"] = trashcan["y"] + 20
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"] + 5, crumpled_pages["size"] + 5, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((204, 204, 204))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(img, layer, f"/sdcard/Download/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/sdcard/Download/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(img)

    pdb.gimp_display_new(img) # Show final frame (optional)
    pdb.gimp_image_delete(img) # Clean up

register(
    "python_fu_donkeyman_meme",
    "Generate Donkeyman Meme Frames",
    "Creates 32 static frames for donkeyman meme",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Donkeyman Meme",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    create_donkeyman_meme,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

### How to Insert and Run on Android GIMP
1. **Locate Script Editor**:
   - Open GIMP on your Android device.
   - Go to `Settings` or `Preferences` and look for a “Scripts,” “Plug-ins,” or “Script-Fu” section. It might be under `Edit > Scripts` or a similar menu.
   - If available, there should be an option to “Add Script” or “Import Script.”

2. **Paste the Script**:
   - Copy the entire Python code above.
   - Paste it into the script editor or import it as a `.py` file if the app allows file uploads (you might need to transfer it via USB or a file manager app).

3. **Adjust Path**:
   - The script saves to `/sdcard/Download/`. On Android, this is typically the Downloads folder. If your GIMP version uses a different path (e.g., internal storage), update the `pdb.file_png_save_defaults` line (e.g., `/sdcard/GIMP/frames/`).
   - Test with one frame to confirm the path works.

4. **Run the Script**:
   - If registered correctly, find it under `Filters > Donkeyman > Donkeyman Meme`.
   - Select an image or let it create a new one, then run. It will generate `frame_01.png` to `frame_32.png` in your Downloads folder.

5. **Check Output**:
   - Use a file manager (e.g., Files by Google) to locate the PNGs. Open a few to verify the donkeyman, "Project Chimera" pages, and trashcan render.

### Converting to .gif
- **On Android**: Use an app like “GIF Maker” or “PhotoGrid” to import the 32 PNGs, set 0.125-second delays, and export as `donkeyman32.gif`.
- **Online**: Upload to Ezgif.com from your phone, adjust timing, and download.
- **Sound**: Add a “paper pile” sound (e.g., from Freesound.org) with `<audio src="paper-pile.mp3" autoplay>` on your webpage.

### Webpage Integration
- **Embed**: `<img src="donkeyman32.gif" alt="Donkeyman trashes Project Chimera">`.
- **Link**: `<a href="project-chimera-details.html">Full Project Chimera Details</a>`.
- **Text**: `<p>This is idiotic—dumping a genius plan!</p><p>Save the assistant, not the trashcan!</p>`.

### Clarification and Support
- **Script Editor Confirmation**: Once GIMP is installed, let me know if you find the script section and its format (e.g., Python or Scheme). I’ll adjust if needed.
- **Path Issues**: If the save path fails, try `/sdcard/DCIM/` or ask GIMP’s export dialog for the default folder.
- **Rendering Problems**: If the output is off (e.g., text missing, shapes wrong), describe the issue, and I’ll tweak the script.
- **Download Progress**: Update me when GIMP is ready, and I’ll guide you through insertion.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

    img = pdb.gimp_image_new(800, 600, RGB)

    pdb.gimp_context_set_background((240, 240, 240))
    pdb.gimp_drawable_fill(layer, BACKGROUND_FILL)

    donkeyman = {"x": 300, "y": 300, "width": 100, "height": 150, "polo_color": (0, 0, 255)}
    server_stack = {"x": 600, "y": 200, "width": 150, "height": 200, "color": (128, 128, 128)}
    trashcan = {"x": 500, "y": 400, "width": 80, "height": 100, "color": (102, 102, 102)}
    pages = {"x": 350, "y": 400, "width": 50, "height": 70, "label": "Project Chimera", "color": (255, 0, 0)}
    crumpled_pages = {"x": 350, "y": 400, "size": 30}

    for frame in range(1, 33):
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(img)
        pdb.gimp_edit_clear(layer)
        pdb.gimp_drawable_fill(layer, BACKGROUND_FILL)

        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, server_stack["x"], server_stack["y"], server_stack["width"], server_stack["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)

        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)

        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, trashcan["x"], trashcan["y"], trashcan["width"], trashcan["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)

        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)

        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"], donkeyman["width"], donkeyman["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
```

Here’s the complete Python-Fu script again, in case you can use one of the workarounds:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

def create_donkeyman_meme():
    img = pdb.gimp_image_new(800, 600, RGB)
    layer = pdb.gimp_layer_new(img, 800, 600, RGB_IMAGE, "Background", 100, NORMAL_MODE)
    pdb.gimp_image_add_layer(img, layer, 0)

        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)

        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
        pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 40, "admin", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 20, "dev", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")

        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)

        if frame <= 4: # Reaching
            pages["x"] = 350 - (frame * 5); pages["y"] = 400 - (frame * 2)
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10, pages["label"], 12, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 8: # Grabbing
            pages["x"] = 320; pages["y"] = 360
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10, pages["label"], 12, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 12: # Starting to crumple
            pages["width"] -= 5; pages["height"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)

            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(img, None, pages["x"] + 5, pages["y"] + 5, pages["label"], 10, True, 0, 0, "Sans")

        elif frame <= 24: # Throwing

            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))

            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(img, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"] + 5, crumpled_pages["size"] + 5, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)

            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(img)

    pdb.gimp_image_delete(img)

register(
    "python_fu_donkeyman_meme",

    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Donkeyman Meme",

    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

### Context and Assumptions
- **Termux Setup**: Your Termux environment is fully equipped with the Android SDK, including `platform-tools` (where ADB resides), Gradle, and build tools. This means `adb` is available, and you can use it to access the GIMP Linux shell.
- **GIMP Linux Shell**: Since GIMP is running in a Linux chroot or container within Android, ADB can connect to its shell (e.g., via `adb shell`) and execute commands in that environment.
- **Script Execution**: We’ll use ADB to push the Python-Fu script to the GIMP environment and run it within GIMP’s Python-Fu interpreter.

### Steps to Port and Run the Script
1. **Verify ADB in Termux**:
   - Open Termux and run `adb version` to confirm ADB is installed. You should see something like `Android Debug Bridge version 1.0.41`.

2. **Connect to the GIMP Linux Shell**:
   - Since GIMP is running in a Linux environment on your device, ADB should detect it as a local device. Run `adb devices` to list connected devices. If GIMP’s environment is isolated, you might need to enable USB debugging on your device and connect it via USB, then switch to TCP/IP mode with `adb tcpip 5555` and connect locally (e.g., `adb connect 127.0.0.1:5555`).

```python
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        # Frame-specific actions

            pages["x"] = 350 - (frame * 5)
            pages["y"] = 400 - (frame * 2)
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(image, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
```

### Execution Commands in Termux
1. **Save the Script**:
   - Use a text editor app to save the script as `/sdcard/Download/donkeyman_meme.py`.

2. **Push the Script**:
   - In Termux, run:
     ```bash
     adb push /sdcard/Download/donkeyman_meme.py /data/local/tmp/
     ```

3. **Run the Script in GIMP**:
   - Enter the GIMP shell via ADB:
     ```bash
     adb shell
     ```
   - Locate the GIMP binary (e.g., `/usr/bin/gimp` or `/data/data/org.gimp/files/usr/bin/gimp`) by running `which gimp` or `find / -name gimp 2>/dev/null`.
   - Execute the script non-interactively:
     ```bash
     gimp -i -b '(python-fu-donkemymeme RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)'
     ```
   - The `-i` flag runs GIMP in non-interactive mode, and `-b` executes the Python-Fu command. Adjust the path to `gimp` and the function name (`python-fu-donkemymeme`) based on your setup.

4. **Check Output**:
   - Look for `frame_01.png` to `frame_32.png` in `/sdcard/Download/`. Use a file manager to verify.

### Troubleshooting
- **ADB Connection**: If `adb devices` shows no devices, ensure USB debugging is enabled (`Settings > About Phone > Tap Build Number 7 times > Developer Options > USB Debugging`) and run `adb tcpip 5555` followed by `adb connect 127.0.0.1:5555`.
- **GIMP Path**: If `gimp` isn’t found, search the filesystem or ask the GIMP UI for its location.
- **Permission Issues**: If the script fails to save, ensure `/sdcard/Download/` is writable (`adb shell ls -l /sdcard/Download`).
- **Error Output**: Run `adb shell gimp -i -b '(python-fu-donkemymeme RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)' 2>&1 | tee output.log` to capture errors.

### Converting to .gif and Webpage
- **.gif**: Use “GIF Maker” on Android or Ezgif.com to create `donkeyman32.gif` from the PNGs (0.125-second delays).
- **Sound**: Add a “paper pile” sound with `<audio src="paper-pile.mp3" autoplay>`.
- **Webpage**: Use `<img src="donkeyman32.gif" alt="Donkeyman trashes Project Chimera">`, `<a href="project-chimera-details.html">Full Project Chimera Details</a>`, and `<p>This is idiotic—dumping a genius plan!</p><p>Save the assistant, not the trashcan!</p>`.

### Revised Context
- **No External Computer**: We’ll rely on Termux to interact with the GIMP Linux environment directly, avoiding ADB’s need for a host PC. This means we’ll use Termux to access the GIMP shell or file system internally.
- **GIMP Linux Shell**: Since GIMP is running in a Linux chroot or container (e.g., via UserLAnd or a similar setup), Termux might have access to it if properly configured.
- **File Access**: We’ll save the script to a location Termux and GIMP can share (e.g., `/sdcard/` or a mounted directory), then execute it within GIMP.

### Steps to Port the Script On-Device
1. **Verify Termux Environment**:
   - Open Termux and run `pkg list-all` to confirm `android-tools` (for ADB) or other utilities are installed. If not, install with `pkg install android-tools`.
   - Check if Termux can access the GIMP Linux shell by running `gimp` directly. If it fails, we’ll need to locate the GIMP binary.

2. **Save the Script in Termux**:
   - Use Termux’s built-in editor (e.g., `nano` or `vim`) to create the script. Install `nano` with `pkg install nano` if needed.
   - Run:
     ```bash
     nano /sdcard/Download/donkeyman_meme.py
     ```
   - Paste the script (below) by typing it manually (since pasting isn’t working in GIMP, we’ll assume the same for Termux initially, but Termux’s keyboard input should allow this). Save with `Ctrl + X`, then `Y`, then `Enter`.

3. **Locate GIMP Binary**:
   - In Termux, run `find / -name gimp 2>/dev/null` to find the GIMP executable (e.g., `/data/data/org.gimp/files/usr/bin/gimp` or `/usr/bin/gimp`).
   - If found, note the path. If not, check the GIMP app’s documentation or settings for the shell command.

4. **Execute the Script in GIMP**:
   - Use Termux to run GIMP in batch mode with the script. Assuming the GIMP binary is at `/data/data/org.gimp/files/usr/bin/gimp`, run:
     ```bash
     /data/data/org.gimp/files/usr/bin/gimp -i -b '(python-fu-donkemymeme RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)'
     ```
   - The `-i` flag runs non-interactively, `-b` executes the Python-Fu command, and `--batch` ensures it exits. Adjust the path based on the `find` result.

### Ported Python-Fu Script
Here’s the script tailored for on-device execution. It’s the same as before but with a focus on compatibility with Termux’s file system access.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

def create_donkeyman_meme(image, drawable):
    if not image:
        image = pdb.gimp_image_new(800, 600, RGB)
        layer = pdb.gimp_layer_new(image, 800, 600, RGB_IMAGE, "Background", 100, NORMAL_MODE)
        pdb.gimp_image_add_layer(image, layer, 0)
    else:
        layer = drawable

    pdb.gimp_context_set_background((240, 240, 240)) # Light gray
    pdb.gimp_drawable_fill(layer, BACKGROUND_FILL)

    donkeyman = {"x": 300, "y": 300, "width": 100, "height": 150, "polo_color": (0, 0, 255)}
    server_stack = {"x": 600, "y": 200, "width": 150, "height": 200, "color": (128, 128, 128)}
    trashcan = {"x": 500, "y": 400, "width": 80, "height": 100, "color": (102, 102, 102)}
    pages = {"x": 350, "y": 400, "width": 50, "height": 70, "label": "Project Chimera", "color": (255, 0, 0)}
    crumpled_pages = {"x": 350, "y": 400, "size": 30}

    for frame in range(1, 33):
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)
        pdb.gimp_edit_clear(layer)
        pdb.gimp_drawable_fill(layer, BACKGROUND_FILL)

        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(image, server_stack["x"], server_stack["y"], server_stack["width"], server_stack["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground(server_stack["color"])
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(image, trashcan["x"], trashcan["y"], trashcan["width"], trashcan["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground(trashcan["color"])
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, trashcan["x"] + 10, trashcan["y"] + 10, "spam", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")

        pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(image, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"], donkeyman["width"], donkeyman["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground(donkeyman["polo_color"])
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 40, "admin", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 20, "dev", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(image, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 40, 30, 30, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        if frame <= 4: # Reaching
            pages["x"] = 350 - (frame * 5); pages["y"] = 400 - (frame * 2)
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(image, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10, pages["label"], 12, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 8: # Grabbing
            pages["x"] = 320; pages["y"] = 360
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(image, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10, pages["label"], 12, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 12: # Starting to crumple
            pages["width"] -= 5; pages["height"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(image, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, pages["x"] + 5, pages["y"] + 5, pages["label"], 10, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 16: # Mid-crumple
            pages["width"] -= 5; pages["height"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(image, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, pages["x"] + 3, pages["y"] + 3, pages["label"], 8, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 20: # Fully crumpled, winding up
            crumpled_pages["size"] = 30
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(image, donkeyman["x"] + 20, donkeyman["y"] + 20, crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            donkeyman["x"] += 10
        elif frame <= 24: # Throwing
            crumpled_pages["x"] = donkeyman["x"] + 30 + ((frame - 20) * 10)
            crumpled_pages["y"] = donkeyman["y"] + 20 - ((frame - 20) * 5)
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(image, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            if frame == 21:
                pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"] - 20, "Bray!", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 28: # Pages soaring
            crumpled_pages["x"] += 20; crumpled_pages["y"] += 5
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(image, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        else: # 29-32: Landing with paper pile effect
            crumpled_pages["x"] = trashcan["x"] + 20; crumpled_pages["y"] = trashcan["y"] + 20
            pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(image, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"] + 5, crumpled_pages["size"] + 5, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((204, 204, 204))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(image, layer, f"/sdcard/Download/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/sdcard/Download/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

    pdb.gimp_display_new(image) # Optional
    pdb.gimp_image_delete(image)

register(
    "python-fu-donkemymeme",
    "Generate Donkeyman Meme Frames",
    "Creates 32 static frames for donkeyman meme",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Donkeyman Meme",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    create_donkeyman_meme,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

### On-Device Execution in Termux
1. **Create the Script**:
   - In Termux, run:
     ```bash
     nano /sdcard/Download/donkeyman_meme.py
     ```
   - Type or paste the script (use a notes app to copy chunks if pasting works there, then type into `nano`). Save with `Ctrl + X`, `Y`, `Enter`.

2. **Find GIMP Path**:
   - Run `find / -name gimp 2>/dev/null` in Termux to locate the GIMP binary (e.g., `/data/data/org.gimp/files/usr/bin/gimp`).
   - If not found, try `which gimp` after activating the GIMP environment (e.g., `source /data/data/org.gimp/files/environment.sh` if it exists).

3. **Run the Script**:
   - Execute:
     ```bash
     /data/data/org.gimp/files/usr/bin/gimp -i -b '(python-fu-donkemymeme RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)'
     ```
   - Replace the path with the correct GIMP location. If the environment needs activation, prepend the source command.

4. **Check Output**:
   - Look for `frame_01.png` to `frame_32.png` in `/sdcard/Download/` using a file manager.

### Troubleshooting
- **No GIMP Binary**: If `find` fails, the Linux environment might not be mounted. Run `mount | grep gimp` or check GIMP’s app settings for a shell command.
- **Permission Denied**: Ensure `/sdcard/Download/` is writable (`ls -l /sdcard/Download` should show `rw`). Use `chmod 777 /sdcard/Download` if needed (with caution).
- **Errors**: Run with logging: `gimp -i -b '(python-fu-donkemymeme RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)' > output.log 2>&1` and share `output.log`.

### Converting to .gif and Webpage
- **.gif**: Use “GIF Maker” or Ezgif

This toggle likely enables support for running a full Linux distro (e.g., via UserLAnd, Termux, or a native feature) alongside Android, which GIMP needs to function with its scripting capabilities. Without it, the installation might be stalling or running in a limited mode, explaining the delay and the inability to paste scripts. Let’s activate that toggle and get everything rolling!

### Steps to Activate the Linux Development Environment
1. **Enable Developer Options**:
   - If not already enabled, go to `Settings > About Phone` on your Google Pixel.
   - Tap `Build Number` 7 times until you see a message like “You are now a developer.”

2. **Access Developer Options**:
   - Go back to `Settings > System > Developer Options`.

3. **Activate Linux Development Environment**:
   - Scroll to find the “Linux development environment” or “Run a Linux environment” toggle. On Pixel devices with Android 13 or later (as of July 31, 2025), this might be under “Networking” or “Experimentation” settings within Developer Options.
   - Enable the toggle. This might prompt you to download a Linux image (e.g., Debian or Ubuntu) and set up a container, which could take a few minutes depending on your internet speed.

4. **Configure the Environment**:
   - Follow any on-screen instructions to set a username and password for the Linux environment.
   - Once configured, it should integrate with Termux or provide a shell accessible via ADB.

5. **Relaunch GIMP**:
   - After enabling the toggle and letting the Linux environment settle, reopen the GIMP app. The installation should complete, and you should see the full interface, including the script editor.

### Updated Plan with ADB and Termux
Since you have Termux with ADB, Gradle, and the Android SDK, we can use this setup once the Linux environment is active:
- **Verify Linux Shell**: After activation, run `adb shell` from Termux to confirm you can access the Linux environment.
- **Push the Script**: Use `adb push /sdcard/Download/donkeyman_meme.py /data/local/tmp/` to transfer the Python-Fu script.
- **Run in GIMP**: Execute `gimp -i -b '(python-fu-donkemymeme RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)'` in the Linux shell.

### Troubleshooting the Linux Development Environment
1. **Check Toggle Activation**:
   - Go back to `Settings > System > Developer Options` and ensure the “Linux development environment” toggle is still on. If it’s off, toggle it on again.
   - Look for a notification or prompt (e.g., “Download Linux image?” or “Set up Linux”). If present, follow the steps to install a Linux distro (e.g., Debian).

2. **Verify Linux Status**:
   - Open Termux and run `adb devices` to see if any devices (including the Linux environment) are detected.
   - Run `adb shell` to enter the shell. If it drops you into a Linux prompt (e.g., `root@pixel:/#`), the environment is active. If it’s still the Android shell, the toggle might need more time or manual configuration.

3. **Manual Linux Setup (if Needed)**:
   - If the toggle didn’t auto-configure, use Termux to set up a Linux environment manually. Run:
     ```bash
     pkg update && pkg upgrade
     pkg install termux-exec
     termux-chroot
     pkg install proot
     termux-chroot proot -0
     ```
   - Then install a distro like Ubuntu:
     ```bash
     pkg install ubuntu
     ubuntu
     ```
   - This creates a chroot environment where GIMP can run. Install GIMP inside it with `apt update && apt install gimp`.

4. **Restart GIMP**:
   - Close and reopen the GIMP app after toggling or setting up the Linux environment. Check if the interface loads fully.

```python
from gimpfu import *

def create_donkeyman_meme(image, drawable):

        image = pdb.gimp_image_new(800, 600, RGB)

        pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 20, "dev", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        pdb.gimp_ellipse_select(image, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] - 40, 30, 30, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
        pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
        pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
        pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        if frame <= 4: # Reaching
            pages["x"] = 350 - (frame * 5)
            pages["y"] = 400 - (frame * 2)
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(image, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, pages["x"] + 10, pages["y"] + 10, pages["label"], 12, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 8: # Grabbing
            pages["x"] = 320; pages["y"] = 360
            pdb.gimp_rectangle_select(image, pages["x"], pages["y"], pages["width"], pages["height"], CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, False, 0)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))

        elif frame <= 28: # Pages soaring

            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
```

### Python-Fu Script for 32 Frames
This script uses the current image, modifies the donkeyman’s expression and actions across 32 frames, and saves each frame. It adjusts the face, crumples the page, and simulates the throw.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

def animate_donkeyman(image, drawable):
    # Use the existing image and layer
    layer = drawable
    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)

    # Define initial donkeyman position and assets
    donkeyman = {"x": 300, "y": 300, "width": 100, "height": 150, "arm_x": 350, "arm_y": 280} # Right arm bent
    page = {"x": 340, "y": 290, "width": 50, "height": 70, "label": "Project Chimera", "color": (255, 0, 0)}
    crumpled_page = {"x": 340, "y": 290, "size": 30}

    # Animation sequence over 32 frames
    for frame in range(1, 33):
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)
        pdb.gimp_edit_copy(layer) # Preserve original layer
        new_layer = pdb.gimp_layer_new_from_drawable(layer, image)
        pdb.gimp_image_add_layer(image, new_layer, 0)

        # Modify donkeyman based on frame
        if frame <= 8: # Smile to stupid face (e.g., widen mouth, skew eyes)
            if frame == 1:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 20, donkeyman["y"] + 20, 20, 20) # Left eye
                pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((0, 0, 0))
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 60, donkeyman["y"] + 20, 20, 20) # Right eye
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] + 70, 40, 20) # Smile
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            elif frame > 4:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 25, donkeyman["y"] + 70, 50, 25) # Widen mouth
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 15, donkeyman["y"] + 15, 30, 30) # Skew left eye
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        elif frame <= 16: # Crumple the page
            page["width"] -= 3; page["height"] -= 3
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, page["x"], page["y"], page["width"], page["height"])
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170)) # Grayed crumpled look
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, page["x"] + 5, page["y"] + 5, page["label"], 10, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 24: # Throw motion (move arm, release page)
            donkeyman["arm_x"] += 10; donkeyman["arm_y"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["arm_x"], donkeyman["arm_y"], 20, 40) # Arm motion
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            crumpled_page["x"] = donkeyman["arm_x"] + 20 + ((frame - 16) * 10)
            crumpled_page["y"] = donkeyman["arm_y"] + 10 - ((frame - 16) * 5) # Arc over shoulder
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            if frame == 21:
                pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"] - 20, "Bray!", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        else: # 25-32: Page flies to background (off-screen for now)
            crumpled_page["x"] += 20; crumpled_page["y"] += 5
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(image, new_layer, f"/sdcard/Download/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/sdcard/Download/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_remove_layer(image, new_layer)
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

register(
    "python-fu-animate-donkeyman",
    "Animate Donkeyman Meme",
    "Animates donkeyman over 32 frames",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Animate Donkeyman",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    animate_donkeyman,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

1. **Save the Script**:

- **Output**: Frames will save to a writable directory (e.g., `/home/user/frames/` or your current working directory).

### Python-Fu Script for 32 Frames
This script modifies the existing image layer across 32 frames, animating the face, crumpling, and throwing sequence. It uses the current layer as the base and saves each frame as a PNG.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

def animate_donkeyman(image, drawable):
    # Use the existing image and layer
    layer = drawable
    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)

    # Define initial donkeyman position and assets
    donkeyman = {"x": 300, "y": 300, "width": 100, "height": 150, "arm_x": 350, "arm_y": 280} # Right arm bent
    page = {"x": 340, "y": 290, "width": 50, "height": 70, "label": "Project Chimera", "color": (255, 0, 0)}
    crumpled_page = {"x": 340, "y": 290, "size": 30}

    # Animation sequence over 32 frames
    for frame in range(1, 33):
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)
        pdb.gimp_edit_copy(layer) # Preserve original
        new_layer = pdb.gimp_layer_new_from_drawable(layer, image)
        pdb.gimp_image_add_layer(image, new_layer, 0)

        # Modify donkeyman based on frame
        if frame <= 8: # Smile to stupid face
            if frame == 1:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 20, donkeyman["y"] + 20, 20, 20) # Left eye
                pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((0, 0, 0))
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 60, donkeyman["y"] + 20, 20, 20) # Right eye
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 30, donkeyman["y"] + 70, 40, 20) # Smile
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            elif frame > 4:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 25, donkeyman["y"] + 70, 50, 25) # Widen mouth
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 15, donkeyman["y"] + 15, 30, 30) # Skew left eye
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        elif frame <= 16: # Crumple the page
            page["width"] -= 3; page["height"] -= 3
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, page["x"], page["y"], page["width"], page["height"])
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170)) # Grayed crumpled look
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, page["x"] + 5, page["y"] + 5, page["label"], 10, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 24: # Throw motion
            donkeyman["arm_x"] += 10; donkeyman["arm_y"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["arm_x"], donkeyman["arm_y"], 20, 40) # Arm motion
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            crumpled_page["x"] = donkeyman["arm_x"] + 20 + ((frame - 16) * 10)
            crumpled_page["y"] = donkeyman["arm_y"] + 10 - ((frame - 16) * 5) # Arc over shoulder
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            if frame == 21:
                pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"] - 20, "Bray!", 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        else: # 25-32: Page flies to background
            crumpled_page["x"] += 20; crumpled_page["y"] += 5
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(image, new_layer, f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_remove_layer(image, new_layer)
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

register(
    "python-fu-animate-donkeyman",
    "Animate Donkeyman Meme",
    "Animates donkeyman over 32 frames",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Animate Donkeyman",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    animate_donkeyman,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

### How to Run the Script
1. **Save the Script**:
   - Since you’re in a Linux environment with full access, use a text editor (e.g., `nano` or `vim`) in the terminal. Run:
     ```bash
     nano /home/user/donkeyman_meme.py
     ```
   - Paste the script, save (`Ctrl+O`, Enter, `Ctrl+X`), and ensure the file is executable:
     ```bash
     chmod +x /home/user/donkeyman_meme.py
     ```

2. **Open GIMP with the Image**:
   - Ensure your donkeyman image is open in GIMP. Launch GIMP if needed:
     ```bash
     gimp
     ```
   - Alternatively, open it via command line:
     ```bash
     gimp /path/to/donkeyman_image.xcf &
     ```

3. **Execute the Script**:
   - Run the script within GIMP:
     ```bash
     gimp -i -b '(python-fu-animate-donkeyman RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)' /path/to/donkeyman_image.xcf
     ```
   - Adjust the path to your image and the script’s location if different from `/home/user/donkeyman_meme.py`.

4. **Check Output**:
   - Navigate to `/home/user/frames/` (create it with `mkdir /home/user/frames` if it doesn’t exist) and verify `frame_01.png` to `frame_32.png` using `ls`.

### Notes on the Animation
- **Face Transformation**: Frames 1–8 morph the smile into a stupid face by widening the mouth and skewing an eye (simplified with selections on the existing layer).
- **Crumpling**: Frames 9–16 shrink and gray the page.
- **Throwing**: Frames 17–24 move the arm and release the crumpled page in an arc.
- **Flight**: Frames 25–32 move the page off-screen toward the background.
- **Background**: Left empty for you to add the office and spam trashcan later.

### Adding the Office Background Later
- **Manual Edit**: Open the frames in GIMP, create a new layer with a gray office background and a black trashcan labeled “spam” (e.g., `pdb.gimp_rectangle_select` for shapes), and merge.
- **Script Extension**: If you want, share your background design, and I’ll update the script.

### Converting to .gif and Webpage
- **.gif**: Use GIMP’s “Export As” with “As Animation” (0.125-second delays) or an online tool like Ezgif.com.
- **Sound**: Add a “paper pile” sound with `<audio src="paper-pile.mp3" autoplay>`.
- **Webpage**: Use `<img src="donkeyman32.gif" alt="Donkeyman trashes Project Chimera">`, `<a href="project-chimera-details.html">Full Project Chimera Details</a>`, and `<p>This is idiotic—dumping a genius plan!</p><p>Save the assistant, not the trashcan!</p>`.

### Troubleshooting
- **Path Issues**: If `/home/user/frames/` isn’t writable, use `/tmp/frames/` or your home directory (check with `pwd`).
- **GIMP Errors**: If the script fails, run it with `gimp -i -b '(python-fu-animate-donkeyman RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)' 2>&1 | tee output.log` to capture errors.
- **Session Drop**: If you log out, the environment might reset. Stay logged in or save your work.

- **Output Check**: After running, let me know if the frames look right (e.g., face change, crumple motion).
- **Background Plan**: Tell me when you’re ready to add the office and trashcan, and I’ll assist.

```python
        elif frame <= 24: # Throw motion
            donkeyman["arm_x"] += 10; donkeyman["arm_y"] -= 5
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["arm_x"], donkeyman["arm_y"], 20, 40)
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)

            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(image, new_layer, f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_remove_layer(image, new_layer)
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

register(

    ],

)

main()
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

def animate_donkeyman(image, drawable):
    layer = drawable
    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)

    # Adjusted for scaled donkeyman (80x120) at new center
    donkeyman = {"x": 310, "y": 310, "width": 80, "height": 120, "arm_x": 360, "arm_y": 290}
    page = {"x": 350, "y": 300, "width": 40, "height": 56, "label": "Project Chimera", "color": (255, 0, 0)}
    crumpled_page = {"x": 350, "y": 300, "size": 24}

    for frame in range(1, 33):
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)
        pdb.gimp_edit_copy(layer)
        new_layer = pdb.gimp_layer_new_from_drawable(layer, image)
        pdb.gimp_image_add_layer(image, new_layer, 0)

        if frame <= 8: # Smile to stupid face
            if frame == 1:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 16, donkeyman["y"] + 16, 16, 16) # Left eye
                pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((0, 0, 0))
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 48, donkeyman["y"] + 16, 16, 16) # Right eye
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 24, donkeyman["y"] + 56, 32, 16) # Smile
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            elif frame > 4:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 20, donkeyman["y"] + 56, 40, 20) # Widen mouth
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 12, donkeyman["y"] + 12, 24, 24) # Skew left eye
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        elif frame <= 16: # Crumple the page
            page["width"] -= 2.4; page["height"] -= 3.5
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, page["x"], page["y"], page["width"], page["height"])
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, page["x"] + 4, page["y"] + 4, page["label"], 8, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 24: # Throw motion
            donkeyman["arm_x"] += 8; donkeyman["arm_y"] -= 4
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["arm_x"], donkeyman["arm_y"], 16, 32)

            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
```

```python
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 48, donkeyman["y"] + 16, 16, 16) # Right eye
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 24, donkeyman["y"] + 56, 32, 16) # Smile
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            elif frame > 4:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 20, donkeyman["y"] + 56, 40, 20) # Widen mouth
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 12, donkeyman["y"] + 12, 24, 24) # Skew left eye
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        elif frame <= 16: # Crumple the page
            page["width"] -= 2.4; page["height"] -= 3.5
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, page["x"], page["y"], page["width"], page["height"])
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, page["x"] + 4, page["y"] + 4, page["label"], 8, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        elif frame <= 24: # Throw motion
            donkeyman["arm_x"] += 8; donkeyman["arm_y"] -= 4
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["arm_x"], donkeyman["arm_y"], 16, 32)
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            crumpled_page["x"] = donkeyman["arm_x"] + 16 + ((frame - 16) * 8)
            crumpled_page["y"] = donkeyman["arm_y"] + 8 - ((frame - 16) * 4)
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            if frame == 21:
                pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"] - 16, "Bray!", 16, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        else: # 25-32: Page flies to background
            crumpled_page["x"] += 16; crumpled_page["y"] += 4
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(image, new_layer, f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_remove_layer(image, new_layer)
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

register(
    "python-fu-animate-donkeyman",
    "Animate Donkeyman Meme",
    "Animates donkeyman over 32 frames",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Animate Donkeyman",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    animate_donkeyman,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

### How to Proceed
1. **Scale the Image (if not done)**:
   - In GIMP, select the donkeyman layer, `Layer > Scale Layer`, set 80%, and save as `donkeyman_scaled.xcf`.

2. **Save the Script**:
   - In your Linux terminal:
     ```bash
     nano /home/user/donkeyman_meme.py
     ```
   - Paste the script, save (`Ctrl+O`, Enter, `Ctrl+X`), and:
     ```bash
     chmod +x /home/user/donkeyman_meme.py
     ```

3. **Run the Script**:
   - Execute:
     ```bash
     gimp -i -b '(python-fu-animate-donkeyman RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)' /home/user/donkeyman_scaled.xcf
     ```
   - Adjust the path if your image is elsewhere.

4. **Check Output**:
   - Verify `/home/user/frames/` for `frame_01.png` to `frame_32.png`. Create it if needed:
     ```bash
     mkdir -p /home/user/frames
     ```

### Analysis and Scaling Suggestion

- **Percentage**: Scaling from an estimated 800x1200 to 400x600 is a 50% reduction. This keeps him visible but allows the middle-distance throw and background elements to fit naturally.

### Updated Python-Fu Script
I’ll adjust the script for the 1000x1500 canvas and 50% scaled donkeyman (400x600 at 300,450). The animation now includes the page turn, slack face with drool, crumpling, and throw:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

def animate_donkeyman(image, drawable):
    layer = drawable
    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)

    # Scaled donkeyman at 50% (400x600) centered lower
    donkeyman = {"x": 300, "y": 450, "width": 400, "height": 600, "arm_x": 500, "arm_y": 500}
    page = {"x": 480, "y": 520, "width": 100, "height": 140, "label": "Project Chimera", "color": (255, 0, 0)}
    crumpled_page = {"x": 480, "y": 520, "size": 50}

    for frame in range(1, 33):
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)
        pdb.gimp_edit_copy(layer)
        new_layer = pdb.gimp_layer_new_from_drawable(layer, image)
        pdb.gimp_image_add_layer(image, new_layer, 0)

        if frame <= 8: # Turn page and slack face
            if frame == 1:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, page["x"], page["y"], page["width"], page["height"])
                pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((255, 255, 255))
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, page["x"] + 20, page["y"] + 20, page["label"], 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
            elif frame > 4:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 150, donkeyman["y"] + 200, 100, 100) # Face area
                pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((200, 200, 200)) # Slack effect
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 160, donkeyman["y"] + 220, 20, 20) # Drool
                pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((150, 150, 150))
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        elif frame <= 16: # Crumple the page
            page["width"] -= 6; page["height"] -= 8
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, page["x"], page["y"], page["width"], page["height"])
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        elif frame <= 24: # Throw motion
            donkeyman["arm_x"] += 40; donkeyman["arm_y"] -= 20
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["arm_x"], donkeyman["arm_y"], 40, 80)
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            crumpled_page["x"] = donkeyman["arm_x"] + 40 + ((frame - 16) * 20)
            crumpled_page["y"] = donkeyman["arm_y"] + 20 - ((frame - 16) * 10) # Middle distance arc
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            if frame == 21:
                pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"] + 150, donkeyman["y"] + 50, "Bray!", 30, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        else: # 25-32: Page flies to middle distance
            crumpled_page["x"] += 40; crumpled_page["y"] += 10
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_page["x"], crumpled_page["y"], crumpled_page["size"], crumpled_page["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(image, new_layer, f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_remove_layer(image, new_layer)
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

register(
    "python-fu-animate-donkeyman",
    "Animate Donkeyman Meme",
    "Animates donkeyman over 32 frames",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Animate Donkeyman",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    animate_donkeyman,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```
