The current size is appropriate for this, so we won’t scale it further. Let’s build this for your 1000x1500 canvas as of 01:16 PM CDT on August 02, 2025.

### Animation Breakdown (64 Frames)
- **Frames 1–16**: Turn the page to face him, flipping through three pages, with his face gradually getting stupider (slack jaw, skewed eyes, drool increasing).
- **Frames 17–32**: Crumple the three pages into a single mass.
- **Frames 33–48**: Throw the crumpled pages over his shoulder into the background, landing where you’ll place the trashcan.
- **Frames 49–64**: Walk off to the right, drooling more as he goes.

### Updated Python-Fu Script (64 Frames)
This script uses the existing 1000x1500 image, animating the donkeyman at his current size (estimated 800x1200), with coordinates adjusted for the larger canvas.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python

from gimpfu import *

def animate_donkeyman(image, drawable):
    layer = drawable
    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)

    # Donkeyman at current size, centered lower on 1000x1500
    donkeyman = {"x": 100, "y": 150, "width": 800, "height": 1200, "arm_x": 300, "arm_y": 300}
    pages = [{"x": 280, "y": 320, "width": 100, "height": 140, "label": "Project Chimera", "color": (255, 0, 0)},
             {"x": 390, "y": 320, "width": 100, "height": 140, "label": "Page 2", "color": (255, 255, 255)},
             {"x": 500, "y": 320, "width": 100, "height": 140, "label": "Page 3", "color": (255, 255, 255)}]
    crumpled_pages = {"x": 280, "y": 320, "size": 50}

    for frame in range(1, 65):
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_start(image)
        pdb.gimp_edit_copy(layer)
        new_layer = pdb.gimp_layer_new_from_drawable(layer, image)
        pdb.gimp_image_add_layer(image, new_layer, 0)

        if frame <= 16: # Turn and flip pages, face gets stupider
            page_idx = min((frame - 1) // 6, 2) # Cycle through 3 pages
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, pages[page_idx]["x"], pages[page_idx]["y"], pages[page_idx]["width"], pages[page_idx]["height"])
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground(pages[page_idx]["color"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, pages[page_idx]["x"] + 20, pages[page_idx]["y"] + 20, pages[page_idx]["label"], 20, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
            if frame > 4:
                pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 300, donkeyman["y"] + 400, 200, 200) # Face area
                pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((200, 200, 200)) # Slack effect
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
                pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 320, donkeyman["y"] + 450, 40 * (frame - 4) / 12, 20 * (frame - 4) / 12) # Increasing drool
                pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((150, 150, 150))
                pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
                pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        elif frame <= 32: # Crumple the pages
            crumple_progress = (frame - 17) / 15
            crumpled_pages["size"] = 50 * (1 - crumple_progress)
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"])
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((170, 170, 170))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
        elif frame <= 48: # Throw over shoulder
            donkeyman["arm_x"] += 40; donkeyman["arm_y"] -= 20
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["arm_x"], donkeyman["arm_y"], 40, 80)
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            crumpled_pages["x"] = donkeyman["arm_x"] + 40 + ((frame - 33) * 20)
            crumpled_pages["y"] = donkeyman["arm_y"] + 20 - ((frame - 33) * 10) # Arc to middle distance
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, crumpled_pages["x"], crumpled_pages["y"], crumpled_pages["size"], crumpled_pages["size"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            if frame == 41:
                pdb.gimp_text_fontname(image, None, donkeyman["x"] + 300, donkeyman["y"] + 100, "Bray!", 40, True, 0, 0, "Sans")
        else: # 49-64: Walk off, drooling
            donkeyman["x"] += 20
            pdb.gimp_image_select_rectangle(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"], donkeyman["y"], donkeyman["width"], donkeyman["height"])
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            drool_size = 40 + ((frame - 49) * 5)
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 320, donkeyman["y"] + 450, drool_size, drool_size / 2)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((150, 150, 150))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(image, new_layer, f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_remove_layer(image, new_layer)
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

register(
    "python-fu-animate-donkeyman",
    "Animate Donkeyman Meme",
    "Animates donkeyman over 64 frames",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Animate Donkeyman",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    animate_donkeyman,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

### How to Run
1. **Save the Script**:
   - Run `nano /home/user/donkeyman_meme.py`, paste the script, save (`Ctrl+O`, Enter, `Ctrl+X`), and:
     ```bash
     chmod +x /home/user/donkeyman_meme.py
     ```

2. **Execute**:
   - Run:
     ```bash
     gimp -i -b '(python-fu-animate-donkeyman RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)' /path/to/your_image.xcf
     ```
   - Replace `/path/to/your_image.xcf` with your file’s path.

3. **Check Output**:
   - Ensure `/home/user/frames/` exists (`mkdir -p /home/user/frames`) and check for `frame_01.png` to `frame_64.png`.

- **Page Turn (1–16)**: Flips through three pages, with the face slackening and drool growing.
- **Crumpling (17–32)**: Combines the pages into a crumpled ball.
- **Throw (33–48)**: Arcs the pages to the middle distance (you’ll place the trashcan where they land, around 700,500).
- **Walk Off (49–64)**: Moves right, with increasing drool.

```python
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)
            drool_size = 20 + ((frame - 49) * 2.5)
            pdb.gimp_image_select_ellipse(image, CHANNEL_OP_REPLACE, donkeyman["x"] + 160, donkeyman["y"] + 225, drool_size, drool_size / 2)
            pdb.gimp_context_set_foreground((150, 150, 150))
            pdb.gimp_edit_fill(new_layer, FOREGROUND_FILL)
            pdb.gimp_selection_none(image)

        # Save frame
        pdb.file_png_save_defaults(image, new_layer, f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png", f"/home/user/frames/frame_{str(frame).zfill(2)}.png")
        pdb.gimp_image_remove_layer(image, new_layer)
        pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

    pdb.gimp_image_undo_group_end(image)

register(
    "python-fu-animate-donkeyman",
    "Animate Donkeyman Meme",
    "Animates donkeyman over 64 frames",
    "Your Name", "Your Name", "2025",
    "Animate Donkeyman",
    "", # No parameters
    [
        (PF_IMAGE, "image", "Input image", None),
        (PF_DRAWABLE, "drawable", "Input drawable", None)
    ],
    [],
    animate_donkeyman,
    menu="<Image>/Filters/Donkeyman"
)

main()
```

### How to Run
1. **Save the Script**:
   - Run `nano /home/user/donkeyman_meme.py`, paste the script, save (`Ctrl+O`, Enter, `Ctrl+X`), and:
     ```bash
     chmod +x /home/user/donkeyman_meme.py
     ```

2. **Execute**:
   - Run:
     ```bash
     gimp -i -b '(python-fu-animate-donkeyman RUN-NONINTERACTIVE)' --batch '(gimp-quit 0)' /path/to/your_scaled_image.xcf
     ```
   - Use the path to your 50% scaled image.

3. **Check Output**:
   - Ensure `/home/user/frames/` exists (`mkdir -p /home/user/frames`) and check for `frame_01.png` to `frame_64.png`.

### Notes on the Animation
- **Page Turn (1–16)**: Flips through three pages, face slackens with growing drool.
- **Crumpling (17–32)**: Combines pages into a crumpled ball.
- **Throw (33–48)**: Arcs to the middle distance (lands around 500,900—place your trashcan there).
- **Walk Off (49–64)**: Moves right with increasing drool.
